# HL7 v2.9 message structure validation ledger

Generated by `codegen/validate/message-ledger.ts v2.9` on 2026-10-30. Do not edit by hand: rerun the script after
changing a schema or a note (see its header for the sources it reads).

## Sources and rule

| Source | Where | Role |
|---|---|---|
| hl7.eu | the printed chapters, `https://www.hl7.eu/HL7v2x/v29/std29/ch<n>.htm`, message tables read as text | Authoritative |
| hl7.org | the official chapter PDFs for ch02, ch03, ch04, ch04a, ch05, ch06, ch07, ch08, ch09, ch10, ch11, ch12, ch13, ch14, ch15, ch16, ch17 (name and SHA-256 in `codegen/validate/sources/v2.9-hl7org.tsv`), converted by `codegen/extract/pdf-chapter.ts`; in the hl7.eu column they replace the hl7.eu page of that chapter | Authoritative |
| hl7.eu list | the message structure list, `https://www.hl7.eu/HL7v2x/v29/hl7v29msgstruct.htm`, printed from the HL7 database: each structure's syntax and its events | Cross-check of the official chapters |
| Caristix | `https://hl7-definition.caristix.com/v2-api/1/HL7v2.9/TriggerEvents/<EVENT>` | Validation reference |
| HAPI | `hapi-structures-v29/.../model/v29/message/<EVENT>.java` and its group classes, with the event map | Cross-check |

Each structure is rendered as the standard's abstract message syntax (one item per line, groups indented,
`<A|B>` for a choice of segments) and compared by shape; group names are each source's own and are not
compared. hl7.eu decides, unless Caristix and HAPI agree with each other against it; with no hl7.eu table,
Caristix and HAPI must agree. **Status** is `ok` when our structure matches the decided shape, `accepted`
when it differs by a recorded, dated decision (`codegen/validate/message-notes.json`), `differs` when it
differs and nothing records why, and `undecided` when the sources disagree and hl7.eu has no table.

In the event table each source cell says whether that source's shape matches ours: ✓ when it does, `differs` when it does not, and — when the source has no table for the event. Every structure with a `differs` cell is drawn side by side below, with the status and the dated reason where one is recorded.

## Structures that differ from a source

### ADT_A05

Status: **accepted** (hl7.org chapter (hl7.eu list differs)). 2026-10-30: The official v2.9 chapter 3 (V29_CH03_PatientAdmin_tc.pdf) prints "[{ PRT }}" and "[{ IAM }}" in this table, unpaired brackets; the ledger's reader of the table nests the rows after them. Our structure reads them as the importer does (optional, repeating; OH3 and REL optional and single by the hand decisions in codegen/extracted/v2.9/overrides.json) and equals hl7.eu's message structure list. Caristix answers HTTP 500 for 2.9 and HAPI has no v2.9 module.

```
ours (v2.9)           hl7.eu            hl7.eu list           
MSH                   MSH               MSH
[{ARV}]               [{ARV}]           [{ARV}]
[{SFT}]               [{SFT}]           [{SFT}]
[UAC]                 [UAC]             [UAC]
EVN                   EVN               EVN
PID                   PID               PID
[PD1]                 [PD1]             [PD1]
[{OH1}]               [{OH1}]           [{OH1}]
[{OH2}]               [{OH2}]           [{OH2}]
[OH3]                 [OH3]             [OH3]
[{OH4}]               [{OH4}]           [{OH4}]
[{ARV}]               [{ARV}]           [{ARV}]
[{ROL}]               [{ROL}]           [{ROL}]
[{PRT}]               [{PRT}]           [{PRT}]
[{NEXT_OF_KIN:}]      [{GROUP:}]        [{NEXT_OF_KIN:}]
  NK1                   NK1               NK1
  [{OH2}]               [{OH2}]           [{OH2}]
  [OH3]                 [OH3]             [OH3]
PV1                   PV1               PV1
[PV2]                 [PV2]             [PV2]
[{ARV}]               [{ARV}]           [{ARV}]
[{ROL}]               [{ROL}]           [{ROL}]
[{PRT}]               [{PRT}]           [{PRT}]
[{DB1}]               [{DB1}]           [{DB1}]
[{OBSERVATION:}]      [{GROUP:}]        [{OBSERVATION:}]
  OBX                   OBX               OBX
  [{PRT}]               [{PRT}]           [{PRT}]
[{AL1}]                 [{AL1}]         [{AL1}]
[{IAM}]                 [GROUP:]        [{IAM}]
[{DG1}]                   {IAM}         [{DG1}]
[DRG]                     [{DG1}]       [DRG]
[{PROCEDURE:}]            [DRG]         [{PROCEDURE:}]

### EHC_E01

Status: **accepted** (hl7.org chapter (hl7.eu list differs)). 2026-10-30: The official v2.9 chapter 16 (V29_CH16_eClaims_tc.pdf) prints INVOICE_INFORMATION_SUBMIT inside "<" ... ">" with no "|": a choice of one alternative. Our structure reads it as that group, as for v2.8 to v2.8.2; the ledger's reader leaves the group out, and hl7.eu's message structure list renders it as a choice of its rows. hl7.eu's message structure list (hl7v29msgstruct.htm) also puts [{ARV}] after MSH, which the official chapter does not print; the official chapter decides. Caristix answers HTTP 500 for 2.9 and HAPI has no v2.9 module.

[{UAC}]                             [{UAC}]           [{SFT}]
INVOICE_INFORMATION_SUBMIT:         IVC               [{UAC}]
  IVC                               [PYE]             <IVC|[|PYE|]|[|{|CTD|}|]|[|AUT|]|[|{|LOC|}|]|[|{|PRT|}|]|[|{|ROL|}|]|{|PRODUCT_SERVICE_SECTION|PSS|{|PRODUCT_SERVICE_GROUP|PSG|[|{|LOC|}|]|[|{|PRT|}|]|[|{|ROL|}|]|[{|PATIENT_INFO|PID|[|{|PRT|}|]|[|PV1|]|[|PV2|]|[|{|ACC|}|]|{|INSURANCE|IN1|[|IN2|]|}|INSURANCE|[{|DIAGNOSIS|DG1|[|{|NTE|}|]|}]|DIAGNOSIS|[|{|OBX|}|]|[|{|PRT|}|]|}]|PATIENT_INFO|{|PRODUCT_SERVICE_LINE_ITEM|PSL|[|{|NTE|}|]|[|{|ADJ|}|]|[|AUT|]|[|{|LOC|}|]|[|{|PRT|}|]|[|{|ROL|}|]|}|PRODUCT_SERVICE_LINE_ITEM|[{|PROCEDURE|PR1|[|{|NTE|}|]|[|{|PRT|}|]|[|{|ROL|}|]|}]|PROCEDURE|[|{|IPR|}|]|}|PRODUCT_SERVICE_GROUP|}|PRODUCT_SERVICE_SECTION>
  [PYE]                             [{CTD}]
  [{CTD}]                           [AUT]
  [AUT]                             [{LOC}]
  [{LOC}]                           [{PRT}]
  [{PRT}]                           [{ROL}]
  [{ROL}]                           {GROUP:}
  {PRODUCT_SERVICE_SECTION:}          PSS
    PSS                               {GROUP:}
    {PRODUCT_SERVICE_GROUP:}            PSG
      PSG                               [{LOC}]
      [{LOC}]                           [{PRT}]
      [{PRT}]                           [{ROL}]
      [{ROL}]                           [{GROUP:}]
      [{PATIENT_INFO:}]                   PID
        PID                               [{PRT}]
        [{PRT}]                           [PV1]
        [PV1]                             [PV2]
        [PV2]                             [{ACC}]
        [{ACC}]                           {GROUP:}
        {INSURANCE:}                        IN1
          IN1                               [IN2]
          [IN2]                           [{GROUP:}]
        [{DIAGNOSIS:}]                      DG1
          DG1                               [{NTE}]
          [{NTE}]                         [{OBX}]
        [{OBX}]                           [{PRT}]
        [{PRT}]                         {GROUP:}
      {PRODUCT_SERVICE_LINE_ITEM:}        PSL
        PSL                               [{NTE}]
        [{NTE}]                           [{ADJ}]
        [{ADJ}]                           [AUT]
        [AUT]                             [{LOC}]
        [{LOC}]                           [{PRT}]
        [{PRT}]                           [{ROL}]
        [{ROL}]                         [{GROUP:}]
      [{PROCEDURE:}]                      PR1
        PR1                               [{NTE}]
        [{NTE}]                           [{PRT}]
        [{PRT}]                           [{ROL}]
        [{ROL}]                         [{IPR}]

### EHC_E15

Status: **accepted** (hl7.org chapter (hl7.eu list differs)). 2026-10-30: The official v2.9 chapter 16 (V29_CH16_eClaims_tc.pdf) prints PAYMENT_REMITTANCE_HEADER_INFO inside "<" ... ">" with no "|": a choice of one alternative. Our structure reads it as that group, as for v2.8 to v2.8.2; the ledger's reader leaves the group out, and hl7.eu's message structure list renders it as a choice of its rows. hl7.eu's message structure list (hl7v29msgstruct.htm) also puts [{ARV}] after MSH, which the official chapter does not print; the official chapter decides. Caristix answers HTTP 500 for 2.9 and HAPI has no v2.9 module.

```
ours (v2.9)                          hl7.eu           hl7.eu list                          
MSH                                  MSH              MSH
[{SFT}]                              [{SFT}]          [{SFT}]
[{UAC}]                              [{UAC}]          [{UAC}]
PAYMENT_REMITTANCE_HEADER_INFO:      PMT              <PMT|PYE>
  PMT                                PYE              [{PAYMENT_REMITTANCE_DETAIL_INFO:}]
  PYE                                [{GROUP:}]         IPR
[{PAYMENT_REMITTANCE_DETAIL_INFO:}]    IPR              IVC
  IPR                                  IVC              {PRODUCT_SERVICE_SECTION:}
  IVC                                  {GROUP:}           PSS
  {PRODUCT_SERVICE_SECTION:}             PSS              {PRODUCT_SERVICE_GROUP:}
    PSS                                  {GROUP:}           PSG
    {PRODUCT_SERVICE_GROUP:}               PSG              {PSL_ITEM_INFO:}
      PSG                                  {GROUP:}           PSL
      {PSL_ITEM_INFO:}                       PSL              [{ADJ}]
        PSL                                  [{ADJ}]  [{ADJUSTMENT_PAYEE:}]
        [{ADJ}]                      [{GROUP:}]         ADJ
[{ADJUSTMENT_PAYEE:}]                  ADJ              [PRT]
  ADJ                                  [PRT]            [ROL]
  [PRT]                                [ROL]
  [ROL]
```

### MFN_M19

Status: **accepted** (hl7.org chapter (hl7.eu list differs)). 2026-10-30: The official v2.9 chapter 8 (V29_CH08_MasterFiles_tc.pdf) prints PACKAGING (PKG) as a group holding one segment ("--- NAME begin" / "--- NAME end" rows), which our structure keeps; the ledger's reader takes it for the segment alone. hl7.eu's message structure list has the group as ours. Caristix answers HTTP 500 for 2.9 and HAPI has no v2.9 module.

```
ours (v2.9)                hl7.eu         hl7.eu list                
MSH                        MSH            MSH
[{SFT}]                    [{SFT}]        [{SFT}]
[UAC]                      [UAC]          [UAC]
MFI                        MFI            MFI
{CONTRACT_RECORD:}         {GROUP:}       {CONTRACT_RECORD:}
  MFE                        MFE            MFE
  CTR                        CTR            CTR
  [{NTE}]                    [{NTE}]        [{NTE}]
  {MATERIAL_ITEM_RECORD:}    {GROUP:}       {MATERIAL_ITEM_RECORD:}
    ITM                        ITM            ITM
    {PURCHASING_VENDOR:}       {GROUP:}       {PURCHASING_VENDOR:}
      VND                        VND            VND
      [{PACKAGING:}]             [{PKG}]        [{PACKAGING:}]
        PKG                                       PKG
```

### OMI_O23

Status: **accepted** (hl7.org chapter (hl7.eu list differs)). 2026-10-30: The official v2.9 chapter 4 (v29_CH04_Orders_tc.pdf) prints "[REL}" in this table, unpaired brackets; the ledger's reader of the table nests the rows after them. Our structure reads them as the importer does (optional, repeating; OH3 and REL optional and single by the hand decisions in codegen/extracted/v2.9/overrides.json) and equals hl7.eu's message structure list. Caristix answers HTTP 500 for 2.9 and HAPI has no v2.9 module.

```
ours (v2.9)                        hl7.eu          hl7.eu list                        
MSH                                MSH             MSH
[{ARV}]                            [{ARV}]         [{ARV}]
[{SFT}]                            [{SFT}]         [{SFT}]
[UAC]                              [UAC]           [UAC]
[{NTE}]                            [{NTE}]         [{NTE}]
[PATIENT:]                         [GROUP:]        [PATIENT:]
  PID                                PID             PID
  [PD1]                              [PD1]           [PD1]
  [{PRT}]                            [{PRT}]         [{PRT}]
  [OCCUPATIONAL_DATA_FOR_HEALTH:]    [GROUP:]        [OCCUPATIONAL_DATA_FOR_HEALTH:]
    [{OH1}]                            [{OH1}]         [{OH1}]
    [{OH2}]                            [{OH2}]         [{OH2}]
    [OH3]                              [OH3]           [OH3]
    [{OH4}]                            [{OH4}]         [{OH4}]
  [{ARV}]                            [{ARV}]         [{ARV}]
  [{NTE}]                            [{NTE}]         [{NTE}]
  [PATIENT_VISIT:]                   [GROUP:]        [PATIENT_VISIT:]
    PV1                                PV1             PV1
    [PV2]                              [PV2]           [PV2]
    [{PRT}]                            [{PRT}]         [{PRT}]
  [{INSURANCE:}]                     [{GROUP:}]      [{INSURANCE:}]
    IN1                                IN1             IN1
    [IN2]                              [IN2]           [IN2]
    [IN3]                              [IN3]           [IN3]
  [GT1]                              [GT1]           [GT1]
  [{AL1}]                            [{AL1}]         [{AL1}]
{ORDER:}                           {GROUP:}        {ORDER:}
  ORC                                ORC             ORC
  [{PRT}]                            [{PRT}]         [{PRT}]
  [{TIMING:}]                        [{GROUP:}]      [{TIMING:}]
    TQ1                                TQ1             TQ1
    [{TQ2}]                            [{TQ2}]         [{TQ2}]
  OBR                                OBR             OBR
  [{NTE}]                            [{NTE}]         [{NTE}]
  [{PRT}]                            [{PRT}]         [{PRT}]
  [CTD]                              [CTD]           [CTD]

  [{IPR}]

### SMD_S32

Status: **accepted** (hl7.org chapter (hl7.eu list differs)). 2026-10-30: The official v2.9 chapter 17 (V29_CH17_MaterialsMngmt_tc.pdf) prints ANTI-MICROBIAL_DEVICE_CYCLE_DATA inside "<" ... ">" with no "|": a choice of one alternative. Our structure reads it as that group, as for v2.8 to v2.8.2; the ledger's reader leaves the group out, and hl7.eu's message structure list renders it as a choice of its rows. Caristix answers HTTP 500 for 2.9 and HAPI has no v2.9 module.

```
ours (v2.9)                        hl7.eu   hl7.eu list        
MSH                                MSH      MSH
[{SFT}]                            [{SFT}]  [{SFT}]
[UAC]                              [UAC]    [UAC]
ANTI_MICROBIAL_DEVICE_CYCLE_DATA:  SDD      <SDD|[|{|SCD|}|]>
  SDD                              [{SCD}]
  [{SCD}]
```
